});\n    }\n\n    particlesRef.current = [...particlesRef.current, ...newParticles];\n  };\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (burstTrigger !== prevBurstTriggerRef.current) {\n currentRadius: settings.particleSize,\n          color,\n          alpha: 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7,\n          friction: 0.96,\n          wanderAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2\n        });\n {\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n      }\n\n      const pX = pointerRef.current.x;\n      const pY = pointerRef.current.y;\n      const pActive = pointerRef.current.active;\n      const strength = settings.attractionStrength * 1.6;\n\n      const survivingParticles: Particle[] = [];\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // バースト専用粒子の寿命処理\n        if (p.burstLife !== undefined) {\n          p.burstLife -= 1;\n          p.alpha = Math.max(0, p.burstLife / 70);\n          p.vx *= p.friction;\n          p.vy *= p.friction;\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          if (p.burstLife > 0) {\n            survivingParticles.push(p);\n          }\n        } else {\n          // 通常パーティクルの物理挙動\n          p.wanderAngle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;\n          const wanderForce = 0.12;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(p.wanderAngle) * wanderForce;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(p.wanderAngle) * wanderForce;\n\n          if (pActive) {\n            const dx = pX - p.x;\n            const dy = pY - p.y;\n            const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq) || 1;\n            const maxDistance = 420;\n\n            if (dist < maxDistance) {\n              const force = (1 - dist / maxDistance) * strength;\n              const angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);\n\n              if (settings.interactionMode === 'attract') {\n                p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * force * 1.2;\n                p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * force * 1.2;\n              } else if (settings.interactionMode === 'repel') {\n                p.vx -= Math.cos(angle) * force * 2.2;\n                p.vy -= Math.sin(angle) * force * 2.2;\n              } else if (settings.interactionMode === 'orbit') {\n                const perpAngle = angle + Math.PI / 2;\n                const orbitSpeed = force * 2.0;\n                p.vx += Math.cos(perpAngle) * orbitSpeed + Math.cos(angle) * force * 0.4;\n                p.vy += Math.sin(perpAngle) * orbitSpeed + Math.sin(angle) * force * 0.4;\n              }\n            }\n          }\n\n          p.vx *= p.friction;\n          p.vy *= p.friction;\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          // 画面端の折り返し\n          if (p.x < -20) p.x = width + 20;\n          if (p.x > width + 20) p.x = -20;\n          if (p.y < -20) p.y = height + 20;\n          if (p.y > height + 20) p.y = -20;\n\n          survivingParticles.push(p);\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル描画\n        const radius = cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    triggerBurstAt(x, y);\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n      active: true\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      ref={containerRef}\n      className=\"canvas-container\"\n      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport { Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { ParticleSettings, DEFAULT_SETTINGS } from './types';\nimport ParticleCanvas from './components/ParticleCanvas';\nimport ControlPanel from './components/ControlPanel';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<ParticleSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);\n  const [burstTrigger, setBurstTrigger] = useState(0);\n  const [fps, setFps] = useState(60);\n\n  const frameCountRef = useRef(0);\n  const lastTimeRef = useRef(performance.now());\n\n  // FPS計測ループ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let animId: number;\n\n    const calculateFps = (now: number) => {\n      frameCountRef.current++;\n      const elapsed = now - lastTimeRef.current;\n\n      if (elapsed >= 500) {\n        setFps(Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / elapsed));\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n        lastTimeRef.current = now;\n      }\n\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(calculateFps);\n    };\n\n    animId = requestAnimationFrame(calculateFps);\n    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleUpdateSettings = (newSettings: Partial<ParticleSettings>) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...newSettings }));\n  };\n\n  const handleTriggerBurst = () => {\n    setBurstTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);\n  };\n\n  const handleReset = () => {\n    setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);\n    setBurstTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* 高精細Canvasパーティクル描画 */}\n      <ParticleCanvas settings={settings} burstTrigger={burstTrigger} />\n\n      {/* 画面オーバーレイUI */}\n      <div className=\"ui-overlay\">\n        {/* 左上ヘッダーバッジ */}\n        <div className=\"header-badge\">\n          <Sparkles size={16} color=\"#38bdf8\" />\n          <span>オーロラ・パーティクル</span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 右上パフォーマンスインジケーター */}\n        <div className=\"stats-overlay\">\n          <div>FPS: {fps}</div>\n          <div>粒子数: {settings.particleCount}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* コントロールパネル */}\n        <ControlPanel\n          settings={settings}\n          onUpdateSettings={handleUpdateSettings}\n          onTriggerBurst={handleTriggerBurst}\n          onReset={handleReset}\n        />\n\n        {/* 下部操作ガイド */}\n        <div className=\"hint-badge\">\n          ポインタ移動 / 指で追従 ・ タップで花火バースト拡散\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps, THEME_CONFIGS, ParticleSettings } from '../types';\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  baseRadius: number;\n  currentRadius: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  friction: number;\n  wanderAngle: number;\n  burstLife?: number;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = ({ settings, burstTrigger }: ParticleCanvasProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean }>({\n    x: 0,\n    y: 0,\n    active: false\n  });\n  const settingsRef = useRef<ParticleSettings>(settings);\n  const prevBurstTriggerRef = useRef(burstTrigger);\n  const sizeRef = useRef<{ width: number; height: number; dpr: number }>({\n    width: window.innerWidth,\n    height: window.innerHeight,\n    dpr: 1\n  });\n\n  // settingsRefを常に最新に保つ（アニメーションループを再起動させないため）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  // マウス/タッチ座標とクリックによるバースト\n  const triggerBurstAt = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {\n    const burstCount = 65;\n    const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n    const theme = THEME_CONFIGS[currentSettings.colorTheme] || THEME_CONFIGS.aurora;\n    const newParticles: Particle[] = [];\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < burstCount; i++) {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speed = 3 + Math.random() * 8;\n      const color = theme.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * theme.colors.length)];\n      newParticles.push({\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius: currentSettings.particleSize * (1.2 + Math.random() * 1.5),\n        currentRadius: currentSettings.particleSize * 2,\n        color,\n        alpha: 1,\n        friction: 0.94,\n        wanderAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n        burstLife: 60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30)\n      });\n    }\n\n    particlesRef.current = [...particlesRef.current, ...newParticles];\n  }, []);\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (burstTrigger !== prevBurstTriggerRef.current) {\n      prevBurstTriggerRef.current = burstTrigger;\n      const { width, height } = sizeRef.current;\n      const cx = pointerRef.current.active ? pointerRef.current.x : width / 2;\n      const cy = pointerRef.current.active ? pointerRef.current.y : height / 2;\n      triggerBurstAt(cx, cy);\n    }\n  }, [burstTrigger, triggerBurstAt]);\n\n  // パーティクル配列の初期化およびカウント同期・テーマ色反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const { width, height } = sizeRef.current;\n    const theme = THEME_CONFIGS[settings.colorTheme] || THEME_CONFIGS.aurora;\n    const targetCount = settings.particleCount;\n    const currentParticles = particlesRef.current.filter((p) => !p.burstLife);\n\n    if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n      const diff = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n      for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++) {\n        const color = theme.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * theme.colors.length)];\n        currentParticles.push({\n          x: Math.random() * width,\n          y: Math.random() * height,\n          vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,\n          vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.5,\n          baseRadius: settings.particleSize * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8),\n          currentRadius: settings.particleSize,\n          color,\n          alpha: 0.3 + Math.random() * 0.7,\n          friction: 0.96,\n          wanderAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2\n        });\n      }\n    } else if (currentParticles.length > targetCount) {\n      currentParticles.length = targetCount;\n    }\n\n    // テーマ色および基本サイズの更新\n    currentParticles.forEach((p) => {\n      p.color = theme.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * theme.colors.length)];\n      p.baseRadius = settings.particleSize * (0.6 + Math.random() * 0.8);\n    });\n\n    // 既存バースト粒子も含めて保持\n    const bursts = particlesRef.current.filter((p) => !!p.burstLife);\n    particlesRef.current = [...currentParticles, ...bursts];\n  }, [settings.particleCount, settings.colorTheme, settings.particleSize]);\n\n  // アニメーションループ & ResizeObserverによるサイズ管理（初回のみセットアップ）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const canvas = canvasRef.current;\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!canvas || !container) return;\n\n    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });\n    if (!ctx) return;\n\n    let animationFrameId: number;\n\n    const updateCanvasDimensions = (rectWidth: number, rectHeight: number) => {\n      const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;\n      const w = Math.max(1, Math.floor(rectWidth));\n      const h = Math.max(1, Math.floor(rectHeight));\n\n      canvas.width = Math.floor(w * dpr);\n      canvas.height = Math.floor(h * dpr);\n      sizeRef.current = { width: w, height: h, dpr };\n\n      // 画面外に出てしまったパーティクルを現在の領域内に安全にクランプ\n      particlesRef.current.forEach((p) => {\n        if (p.x > w + 20) p.x = Math.random() * w;\n        if (p.y > h + 20) p.y = Math.random() * h;\n      });\n    };\n\n    const initialRect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n    updateCanvasDimensions(initialRect.width, initialRect.height);\n\n    const resizeObserver = new ResizeObserver((entries) => {\n      for (const entry of entries) {\n        const { width, height } = entry.contentRect;\n        if (width > 0 && height > 0) {\n          updateCanvasDimensions(width, height);\n        }\n      }\n    });\n    resizeObserver.observe(container);\n\n    const render = () => {\n      const { width, height, dpr } = sizeRef.current;\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n\n      ctx.save();\n      ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);\n\n      // トレイル（残像）フェード描画\n      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';\n      const trailAlpha = Math.max(0.04, Math.min(0.6, 1 - currentSettings.trailLength));\n      ctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8, 9, 15, ${trailAlpha})`;\n      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);\n\n      // 光彩（発光）ブラー合成モード\n      if (currentSettings.glowEffect) {\n        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'lighter';\n      }\n\n      const pX = pointerRef.current.x;\n      const pY = pointerRef.current.y;\n      const pActive = pointerRef.current.active;\n      const strength = currentSettings.attractionStrength * 1.6;\n\n      const survivingParticles: Particle[] = [];\n\n      for (let i = 0; i < particlesRef.current.length; i++) {\n        const p = particlesRef.current[i];\n\n        // バースト専用粒子の寿命処理\n        if (p.burstLife !== undefined) {\n          p.burstLife -= 1;\n          p.alpha = Math.max(0, p.burstLife / 70);\n          p.vx *= p.friction;\n          p.vy *= p.friction;\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          if (p.burstLife > 0) {\n            survivingParticles.push(p);\n          }\n        } else {\n          // 通常パーティクルの物理挙動\n          p.wanderAngle += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4;\n          const wanderForce = 0.12;\n          p.vx += Math.cos(p.wanderAngle) * wanderForce;\n          p.vy += Math.sin(p.wanderAngle) * wanderForce;\n\n          if (pActive) {\n            const dx = pX - p.x;\n            const dy = pY - p.y;\n            const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;\n            const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq) || 1;\n            const maxDistance = 420;\n\n            if (dist < maxDistance) {\n              const force = (1 - dist / maxDistance) * strength;\n              const angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx);\n\n              if (currentSettings.interactionMode === 'attract') {\n                p.vx += Math.cos(angle) * force * 1.2;\n                p.vy += Math.sin(angle) * force * 1.2;\n              } else if (currentSettings.interactionMode === 'repel') {\n                p.vx -= Math.cos(angle) * force * 2.2;\n                p.vy -= Math.sin(angle) * force * 2.2;\n              } else if (currentSettings.interactionMode === 'orbit') {\n                const perpAngle = angle + Math.PI / 2;\n                const orbitSpeed = force * 2.0;\n                p.vx += Math.cos(perpAngle) * orbitSpeed + Math.cos(angle) * force * 0.4;\n                p.vy += Math.sin(perpAngle) * orbitSpeed + Math.sin(angle) * force * 0.4;\n              }\n            }\n          }\n\n          p.vx *= p.friction;\n          p.vy *= p.friction;\n          p.x += p.vx;\n          p.y += p.vy;\n\n          // 画面端の折り返し\n          if (p.x < -20) p.x = width + 20;\n          if (p.x > width + 20) p.x = -20;\n          if (p.y < -20) p.y = height + 20;\n          if (p.y > height + 20) p.y = -20;\n\n          survivingParticles.push(p);\n        }\n\n        // パーティクル描画\n        const radius = Math.max(0.8, p.baseRadius);\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;\n        ctx.fill();\n\n        // 発光オーラ\n        if (currentSettings.glowEffect && radius > 1.8) {\n          ctx.beginPath();\n          ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, radius * 2.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n          ctx.fillStyle = p.color;\n          ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha * 0.22;\n          ctx.fill();\n        }\n      }\n\n      ctx.restore();\n      particlesRef.current = survivingParticles;\n      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n    };\n\n    animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);\n\n    return () => {\n      resizeObserver.disconnect();\n      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const x = e.clientX - rect.left;\n    const y = e.clientY - rect.top;\n    pointerRef.current = { x, y, active: true };\n    triggerBurstAt(x, y);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerMove = (e: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();\n    pointerRef.current = {\n      x: e.clientX - rect.left,\n      y: e.clientY - rect.top,\n      active: true\n    };\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = () => {\n    pointerRef.current.active = false;\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      ref={containerRef}\n      className=\"canvas-container\"\n      onPointerDown={handlePointerDown}\n      onPointerMove={handlePointerMove}\n      onPointerUp={handlePointerUp}\n      onPointerLeave={handlePointerLeave}\n    >\n      <canvas ref={canvasRef} />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ParticleCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

lastTimeRef.current;\n\n      if (elapsed >= 500) {\n        setFps(Math.round((frameCountRef.current * 1000) / elapsed));\n        frameCountRef.current = 0;\n        lastTimeRef.current = now;\n      }\n\n      animId = requestAnimationFrame(calculateFps);\n    };\n\n    animId = requestAnimationFrame(calculateFps);\n    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(animId);\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"stats-overlay\">\n      <div>FPS: {fps}</div>\n      <div>粒子数: {particleCount}</div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<ParticleSettings>(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);\n  const [burstTrigger, setBurstTrigger] = useState(0);\n\n  const handleUpdateSettings = useCallback((newSettings: Partial<ParticleSettings>) => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...newSettings }));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleTriggerBurst = useCallback(() => {\n    setBurstTrigger((prev) => prev + 1);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleReset = useCallback(() => {\n    setSettings(DEFAULT_SETTINGS);\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      {/* 高精細Canvasパーティクル描画 */}\n      <ParticleCanvas settings={settings} burstTrigger={burstTrigger} />\n\n      {/* 画面オーバーレイUI */}\n      <div className=\"ui-overlay\">\n        {/* 左上ヘッダーバッジ */}\n        <div className=\"header-badge\">\n          <Sparkles size={16} color=\"#38bdf8\" />\n          <span>オーロラ・パーティクル</span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* 右上パフォーマンスインジケーター（独立レンダリング） */}\n        <StatsOverlay particleCount={settings.particleCount} />\n\n        {/* コントロールパネル */}\n        <ControlPanel\n          settings={settings}\n          onUpdateSettings={handleUpdateSettings}\n          onTriggerBurst={handleTriggerBurst}\n          onReset={handleReset}\n        />\n\n        {/* 下部操作ガイド */}\n        <div className=\"hint-badge\">\n          ポインタ移動 / 指で追従 ・ タップで花火バースト拡散\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

type ColorTheme = 'neon' | 'sakura' | 'fire' | 'aurora' | 'cyber';\n\n/**\n * マウスやタッチポインタに対するパーティクルの物理挙動モード\n * - attract: ポインタに向かって吸い寄せられる引力\n * - repel: ポインタから逃げるように散る反発力\n * - orbit: ポインタの周囲を公転・周回する衛星挙動\n */\nexport type InteractionMode = 'attract' | 'repel' | 'orbit';\n\n/**\n * パーティクルアニメーションの全体設定パラメータ\n */\nexport interface ParticleSettings {\n  /**\n   * 画面上に同時に存在するパーティクルの最大総数\n   * @type {number} 100 〜 1500\n   */\n  particleCount: number;\n\n  /**\n   * 粒子の配色パレットテーマ\n   */\n  colorTheme: ColorTheme;\n\n  /**\n   * キャンバス描画時の残像の長さ（1に近いほど長い軌跡が残り、0に近いほど即座に消去）\n   * @type {number} 0.02 〜 0.92\n   */\n  trailLength: number;\n\n  /**\n   * パーティクルの基本半径（px単位）\n   * @type {number} 1.0 〜 5.0\n   */\n  particleSize: number;\n\n  /**\n   * ポインタに対する引力・斥力・公転力の強さの係数\n   * @type {number} 0.2 〜 2.0\n   */\n  attractionStrength: number;\n\n  /**\n   * ポインタに対するインタラクション物理モード\n   */\n  interactionMode: InteractionMode;\n\n  /**\n   * 描画時に加算合成（'lighter'）と光彩オーラ効果を付与するかどうかのフラグ\n   */\n  glowEffect: boolean;\n}\n\n/**\n * スライダー等の数値設定用の最小値・最大値・ステップ幅の制約定義\n */\nexport interface SettingLimit {\n  min: number;\n  max: number;\n  step: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * 各数値パラメータの設定可能レンジ定義\n */\nexport const SETTING_LIMITS: {\n  particleCount: SettingLimit;\n  particleSize: SettingLimit;\n  attractionStrength: SettingLimit;\n  trailLength: SettingLimit;\n} = {\n  particleCount: {\n    min: 100,\n    max: 1500,\n    step: 50\n  },\n  particleSize: {\n    min: 1.0,\n    max: 5.0,\n    step: 0.2\n  },\n  attractionStrength: {\n    min: onUpdateSettings: (newSettings: Partial<ParticleSettings>) => void;\n  onTriggerBurst: () => void;\n  onReset: () => void;\n}\n\n/**\n * テーマごとのカラーパレット設定\n */\nexport interface ThemeConfig {\n  id: }\n};\n\n/**\n * アプリ起動時およびリセット時のデフォルト設定値\n */\nexport const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: ParticleSettings = {\n  particleCount: 500,\n  colorTheme: 'aurora',\n  trailLength: 0.18,\n  particleSize: 2.2,\n  attractionStrength: 0.8,\n  interactionMode: 'attract',\n  glowEffect: true\n};","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/ParticleCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { ParticleCanvasProps, THEME_CONFIGS, ParticleSettings } from '../types';\n\ninterface Particle {\n  x: number;\n  y: number;\n  vx: number;\n  vy: number;\n  baseRadius: number;\n  currentRadius: number;\n  color: string;\n  alpha: number;\n  friction: number;\n  wanderAngle: number;\n  burstLife?: number;\n}\n\nconst ParticleCanvas = ({ settings, burstTrigger }: ParticleCanvasProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const canvasRef = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);\n  const particlesRef = useRef<Particle[]>([]);\n  const pointerRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number; active: boolean }>({\n    x: 0,\n    y: 0,\n    active: false\n  });\n  const settingsRef = useRef<ParticleSettings>(settings);\n  const prevBurstTriggerRef = useRef(burstTrigger);\n  const sizeRef = useRef<{ width: number; height: number; dpr: number }>({\n    width: window.innerWidth,\n    height: window.innerHeight,\n    dpr: 1\n  });\n\n  // settingsRefを常に最新に保つ（アニメーションループを再起動させないため）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  // マウス/タッチ座標とクリックによるバースト\n  const triggerBurstAt = useCallback((x: number, y: number) => {\n    const burstCount = 65;\n    const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n    const theme = THEME_CONFIGS[currentSettings.colorTheme] || THEME_CONFIGS.aurora;\n    const newParticles: Particle[] = [];\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < burstCount; i++) {\n      const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;\n      const speed = 3 + Math.random() * 8;\n      const color = theme.colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * theme.colors.length)];\n      newParticles.push({\n        x,\n        y,\n        vx: Math.cos(angle) * speed,\n        vy: Math.sin(angle) * speed,\n        baseRadius: currentSettings.particleSize * (1.2 + Math.random() * 1.5),\n        currentRadius: currentSettings.particleSize * 2,\n        color,\n        alpha: 1,\n        friction: 0.94,\n        wanderAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n        burstLife: 60 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30)\n      });\n    }\n\n    particlesRef.current = [...particlesRef.current, ...newParticles];\n  }, []);\n\n  // 外部からのバーストトリガー\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (burstTrigger !== prevBurstTriggerRef.current) {\n      prevBurstTriggerRef.current = burstTrigger;\n      const { width, height } = sizeRef.current;\n      const cx = pointerRef.current.active ? pointerRef.current.x : width / 2;\n      const cy = pointerRef.current.active ? pointerRef.current.y : height / 2;\n      triggerBurstAt(cx, cy);\n    }\n  }, [burstTrigger, triggerBurstAt]);\n\n  // パーティクル配列の初期化およびカウント同期・テーマ色反映\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const { width, height } = sizeRef.current;\n    const theme = THEME_CONFIGS[settings.colorTheme] || THEME_CONFIGS.aurora;\n    const targetCount = settings.particleCount;\n    const currentParticles = particlesRef.current.filter((p) => !p.burstLife);\n\n    if (currentParticles.length < targetCount) {\n      const diff = targetCount - currentParticles.length;\n      for (let i = 0; i < diff; i++)
